# RDNA2 instruction reference (gfx10.3 / gfx1031)

Generated from the AMD machine-readable ISA specification, `amdgpu_isa_rdna2.xml`
(MIT, Copyright (c) 2026 Advanced Micro Devices, Inc.).

1153 instructions, grouped by functional unit. Each row lists the primary
encoding, its opcode, and the destination/source fields. Instructions with several
encodings (for example VOP1 and VOP3 forms) show all of them.

## EXPORT (1)

| instruction | encodings | operands | description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `EXP` | `ENC_EXP:0` | TGT, VSRC0, VSRC1, VSRC2, VSRC3, OPR_SDST_EXEC64 | Export graphics data to the next stage of the render pipeline. The target and up to four channels of data are specified as operands. |

## MESSAGE (3)

| instruction | encodings | operands | description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `S_ENDPGM` | `ENC_SOPP:1` |  | End of program; terminate wavefront. |
| `S_ENDPGM_ORDERED_PS_DONE` | `ENC_SOPP:30` |  | End of program; signal that a wave has exited its POPS critical section and terminate wavefront. |
| `S_ENDPGM_SAVED` | `ENC_SOPP:27` |  | End of program; signal that a wave has been saved by the context-switch trap handler and terminate wavefront. |

## WAVE_CONTROL (14)

| instruction | encodings | operands | description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `S_CLAUSE` | `ENC_SOPP:33` | SIMM16 | Mark the beginning of a clause. |
| `S_INST_PREFETCH` | `ENC_SOPP:32` | SIMM16 | Change instruction prefetch mode. This controls how many cachelines ahead of the current PC the shader attempts to prefetch. |
| `S_NOP` | `ENC_SOPP:0` | SIMM16 | Do nothing. Delay issue of next instruction by a small, fixed amount. |
| `S_SETHALT` | `ENC_SOPP:13` | SIMM16 | Set or clear the HALT or FATAL_HALT status bits. |
| `S_SETKILL` | `ENC_SOPP:11` | SIMM16 | Kill this wave if the least significant bit of the immediate constant is 1. |
| `S_SETPRIO` | `ENC_SOPP:15` | SIMM16 | Change wave user priority. |
| `S_SLEEP` | `ENC_SOPP:14` | SIMM16 | Cause a wave to sleep for up to ~8000 clocks. |
| `S_VERSION` | `ENC_SOPK:1` | SIMM16 | Perform no operation. This opcode is used to specify the microcode version for tools that interpret shader microcode. |
| `S_WAITCNT` | `ENC_SOPP:12` | SIMM16 | Wait for the counts of outstanding local data share, vector memory and export instructions to be at or below the specified levels. |
| `S_WAITCNT_DEPCTR` | `ENC_SOPP:35` | SIMM16 | Wait for one or more ALU-centric counters to fall below specified values. |
| `S_WAITCNT_EXPCNT` | `ENC_SOPK:25` | SDST, SIMM16 | Wait for the EXPCNT counter to be at or below the specified level. The EXPCNT counter tracks the number of outstanding export events. |
| `S_WAITCNT_LGKMCNT` | `ENC_SOPK:26` | SDST, SIMM16 | Wait for the LGKMCNT counter to be at or below the specified level. The LGKMCNT counter tracks the number of outstanding local data share (L), global data share (G), scalar memory (K) and message (M) events. |
| `S_WAITCNT_VMCNT` | `ENC_SOPK:24` | SDST, SIMM16 | Wait for the VMCNT counter to be at or below the specified level. The VMCNT counter tracks the number of outstanding vector memory loads and atomics that do return data. When in 'all-in-order' mode, wait for all load and store vector memory events. |
| `S_WAITCNT_VSCNT` | `ENC_SOPK:23` | SDST, SIMM16 | Wait for the VSCNT counter to be at or below the specified level. The VSCNT counter tracks the number of outstanding vector memory stores and atomics that do not return data. This counter is not used in 'all-in-order' mode. |
